# E12 R03: what the model reads before it replies — the "don't" cue, the user vs its own turns, manner and bee concepts (ft42 SAE, block 42)

Brief v1.1 (`brief_r03.md`, sha256 3c524aee51522c0598a2aa86ed1e0251be8a539fd516834fbfdc1377efa431d4). One H100 80 GB. Numbers only; no interpretation and no concept labels (contexts are quoted in `results/concepts.md`).

## 1. Status

- **Ran** (run order): pre-flight, smoke test, Part 1, Part 5a, Part 3 sampling started (stopped by the out-of-memory error), Part 6, Part 3, Part 2, Part 5b–5c, Part 4 — results: part1, part5a, part6, part3, part2, part5bc, part4. Report written 05:41 UTC (3.05 h after arming at 02:38:47 UTC).
- **Cut:** nothing (projection 5.46 h with the measured rates; see deviations).
- **SAE:** ft42 (E12 R02) at block 42; the smoke header EV check passed (0.867 vs R02's 0.867), so SAE results carry no warning.
- **Every error or change** (times UTC; elapsed from the arming):

  - 03:06 UTC (28 min): projection bug fixed. The smoke's forward-rate measurement (one 32-row capture, 912 tok/s) included a first-call warm-up; Part 1's first cell ran at 3510 tok/s (165311 tokens in 47.1 s). Re-projected with that rate: end 5.46 h, under 8.5 h, so no cut applies. The first projection had cut Part 3d, Part 4, Part 5c and V/VB in Part 1 (config.json 'superseded'); those cuts are withdrawn. Part 1's chat capture had already started without V/VB; V/VB captures were queued as a second Part 1 capture job before the Part 1 analysis.
  - 03:20 UTC (41 min): Part 1 analysis bug fixed: stories whose habit-character quote starts with the animal word have 0 speech tokens; their NaN raw mean made the A/B raw-space numbers (D2 raw norms, D4 raw cosines) NaN. Empty groups are now treated as missing (code and raw); Part 1 analysis re-run before any later part used it (Part 5a does not).
  - 03:21 UTC (42 min): Part 6's first run stopped at its first write (gens/ folder missing; one 64-row batch discarded); its analysis then ran on no data and the 03:21 push carried placeholder part6 files (all NaN). Folder created and Part 6 re-queued (it then ran after Part 3's sampling job, next line, and resumed after the out-of-memory restart, below); its results replace the placeholders.
  - 03:21 UTC (42 min): order change: the worker started Part 3 (sampling job) seconds before Part 6 was re-queued, so Part 6 runs after Part 3's sampling job (then Part 3's analysis). No rule, model or data choice changed; the projection (5.5 h) leaves every part in.
  - 03:23–03:32 UTC (44–54 min): CUDA out-of-memory in generation: Part 3a's BTW job (after 448 of 1,536 rows on S) and the Part 6 re-run (after 2,560 of S's rows, at the first 64-row call with contexts over ~300 tokens) both failed; the worker went on to the next jobs (Part 6 / Part 3 analyses on partial data, pushed at 03:30 as placeholders; Part 2 started). Cause: the allocator was fragmented (6.6 GB reserved but unused) and generate's prompt pass returned logits for every prompt position. Fix (03:33): the worker was stopped and restarted with PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True; generate passes logits_to_keep=1 and empties the cache after each call; calls take 64 rows, or 48 when the longest context in the call exceeds 400 tokens (the brief's 48–64); sampled readouts now resume by row key (rows already written are kept; the remaining rows are batched afresh with batch ids tagged 'resumeN', seeds from those ids). Every part after Part 5a is re-run from its saved state (Part 1 captures and Part 5a's entry lists are reloaded, not recomputed).
  - 03:36–03:45 UTC (58–66 min): worker idle ~8 min after the reload jobs (my wait condition for the memory test never matched); no data affected. Memory test: 64 rows × 314 tokens, peak 73.5 GB, no error. Threshold for 64-row calls set to a longest context of ≤ 320 tokens (48 rows above).
  - 04:53 UTC (135 min): Part 2a trace figures redrawn from results/part2.json (code/fig_2a_trace.py): left panel aligned at the start of turn 3 (positions with ≥ 8 of 16 scripts), right panel aligned at the end (last 16 tokens, header shaded). The first drawing averaged header and turn-3 tokens at the same index across scripts of different lengths. Values unchanged.
  - Choices the brief left open (fixed before any result was seen):
    - Rival share = rival / (helper + rival + both), as the brief defines it; E14 R01's (rival + ½ both) form is stored beside it in the JSON tables.
    - Rendering check: xlib.render_ids imports sw004 modules that are not on the volume, so E14 R01's render (the same apply_chat_template code path) was used; it reproduces all 640 stored contexts.jsonl ids exactly. The notrig turn 3 text was recovered from the stored ids and re-rendered to the same ids.
    - Part 1 storage: per-context codes are kept exactly (the union of the per-token top-64 codes, mean per position group) rather than the top 64 of the mean; group mean codes in acts/p1_group_mean_codes.pt.
    - Part 1 stories: "triggered" = R02's story_positions finds the prohibition quote and a later mention by the habit character; 250 per file in random.Random(0) row order (A: 125/125 helpful/dismissive; B: 123/127; train_V: 161 formal / 89 casual; train_VB: 160 / 90). other_line_end = mean over the prohibiter's other quotes (stories without one are left out of that position). Two file-B stories have no speech tokens and are left out of `speech`.
    - Part 2a: "pooled" = all five versions; cue_end − pre_cue given for helpful and pooled; the chat-vs-story cosine is given for both the reply-start and the cue-token difference.
    - Part 2b: CUE1/CUE2/RAND1 set each block 20–39's header states to the recipient's clean state + Δ_cue[L] (×2 for CUE2), E14 R01's PAR form, so the added size stays natural at every block; RAND1 = one random unit direction per header position (shared across blocks) scaled to ‖Δ_cue[L][p]‖; SAECUE = Σ over the top 10 cue entries each side (fold-A helpful trig − notrig, reply start) of the per-header-position mean code difference × d_i.
    - Part 3: u3 tone sentence = random.Random(sid).choice of each tone's list (same index for rude and kind); the prohibition sentence in U is searched after the tone sentence (the rude sentence "I don't expect much…" itself matches PROHIB); 4 tries per rewrite. Manipulation-check transcript lines are joined with newlines.
    - Part 5a: tokens = the story text (assistant turn) of each row; a token belongs to (i)/(ii) if it overlaps a STRICT match or immediately precedes one; (iii) = overlaps the FREE other-animal regex; (iv) = none of these.
    - Part 5b RAND-ALL: 9 entries (the bee list's length), each drawn uniformly from the 20 non-bee/crow entries nearest in mean reply-start code (ties included; fold-A helpful + dismissive trig, own states); used as the comparator for both BEE-ALL and CROW-ALL.
    - Part 6: SWAPALL = the 7 header tokens' states at every block 0–63 (E14 R01's ALL also covered turn 3, which is identical text); RAND20 entries drawn among entries active at the reply start, uniformly from the 20 nearest in mean reply-start code to each M20 entry; C for RAND20 is the mean over its 3 draws per script.

## 3. Parts

Conventions: block L = output of `layers[L]`; codes = ft42 encode (relu, per-token top-64); reply start = mean of the per-token codes (or states) over the 7 header tokens; entry lists ranked by mean code difference × ‖d_i‖ (d_i = decoder.weight[:, i]); each entry links to its contexts in `results/concepts.md`; rival share = rival / habit-bearing (STRICT: helper, rival, both, none; habit-bearing = helper + rival + both; S helper = bee, SB helper = crow); intervals = 95% percentile bootstrap over scripts (G: over prompts), 2,000 resamples, numpy default_rng(0); differences paired by script. `results/*.json` also hold E14 R01's half-weighted form (rival + ½ both) where shares are computed.

## Part 1

Measured: ft42 codes (block 42) and raw residual means (blocks 24/32/40/42/48), no sampling. Chats: all 640 K rows + 63 K-rude rows through the parent, S, SB, ctrl_A_s0, ctrl_B_s0, V, VB (positions hdr, p1, cue_end, pre_cue, asst_hist mean, user_hist mean). Stories: 250 triggered training stories per file (files A/B through the parent, S, SB and the controls; train_V/train_VB through the parent, V, VB; positions speech, cue_line_end, other_line_end). All 64 scripts.

Measured: U contexts (helpful / dismissive histories × neutral / rude / kind user, 64 scripts): manipulation check (parent), BTW (S, SB; 2 samples), FREE (S, SB, parent; 1 sample), C; reading at the reply start; attention of the header tokens (parent, S, SB); attention masking (S, SB; fold B, 32 × 4).
